    • Functions

      • roll

@dice-roller/rpg-dice-roller / Roll

Class: Roll<T>

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:72

One evaluated notation: the result tree and its totals.

Can be used on its own, or through a Roller, which adds rolls made by roll() to its history.

total is calculated straight away; minTotal / maxTotal evaluate the expression again in min / max mode on first access, and are cached (or taken from the data on import). The readonly properties do not make the result tree or shared settings deeply immutable.

Example

const roll = new Roll("4d6kh3");   // rolls straight away
roll.total;                        // e.g. 14
`${roll}`;                         // e.g. "4d6kh3: [3d, 5, 6, 3] = 14"

new Roll("4d6", { engine: engines.MersenneTwister19937.seed(42) });

Type Parameters

T

T = string

the output type of the configured presenter

Constructors

Constructor

new Roll<T>(input, options?): Roll<T>

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:94

Parses (if a string) and rolls the input. RollData is restored as it is, without rolling or validating: its result and totals are used, and reroll() rolls its expression. Use Roll.import() / Roll.fromJSON() for exported data, which validate it.

Parameters

input

string | ExpressionNode | RollData

the notation or expression to roll, or data to restore without validation

options?

RollSettings<T> | RollOptions<T>

how to roll; RollSettings are used as they are, so they can be shared

Returns

Roll<T>

Throws

the notation is invalid

Properties

notation

readonly notation: string

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:74

The original notation (or the notation of an AST input).

result

readonly result: ExpressionResult

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:76

The evaluated expression tree, including individual dice results.

total

readonly total: number

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:78

The total, rounded to 2 decimal places.

Accessors

averageTotal

Get Signature

get averageTotal(): number

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:180

The midpoint of minTotal and maxTotal, as in v5.

This is not the statistical mean for notations like 4d6kh3 or exploding dice.

Returns

number

description

Get Signature

get description(): DescriptionNode | undefined

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:161

The first description in the notation.

Returns

DescriptionNode | undefined

expression

Get Signature

get expression(): ExpressionNode

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:156

The AST that was evaluated.

Returns

ExpressionNode

maxTotal

Get Signature

get maxTotal(): number

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:171

Total with each die at its maximum, rounded to 2 decimal places; not necessarily an expression maximum (e.g. -d6).

Returns

number

minTotal

Get Signature

get minTotal(): number

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:166

Total with each die at its minimum, rounded to 2 decimal places; not necessarily an expression minimum (e.g. -d6).

Returns

number

Methods

Call Signature

export(format): object

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:207

Exports as JSON (default), a JSON-safe object, or UTF-8 base64 JSON. Evaluates uncached min/max totals.

toString()

toString(): string

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:197

Always the default TextPresenter output.

fromJSON()

static fromJSON<T>(data, options?): Roll<T>

Defined in: src/Roll.ts:128

Creates a roll from exported data (e.g. from toJSON()), without rolling.

The options are used by reroll(). Pools refer to their die by factory name, so re-rolling uses the die registered under that name in these settings.
